# Wand of Healing
Aliases: Wand Of Healing
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Zombie Slayer, Wands, Healing Wand, Support Items
[Wand of Healing]
Type: Wand
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Zombie Slayer I (1) ([[Zombie Slayer]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Wand of Mending]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 16x [[Revenant Flesh]] 1x [[Stick]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 1 [[Stick]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WAND_OF_HEALING
Tooltip: Ability: Small Heal RIGHT CLICK Heal 60❤ per second for 5s.; Wand heals don't stack.; Vitality Cost: 25 Cooldown: 1s; ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 1.; UNCOMMON WAND
The Wand of Healing is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Wands|Healing Wand]] and [[Support Items|Support Item]]. It can be upgraded into the [[Wand of Mending]], followed by the [[Wand of Restoration]] and [[Wand of Atonement]]. It can only be crafted and used after reaching [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer I (1)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Zombie Slayer I (1) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Stick]] 16x [[Revenant Flesh]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Flesh]] x8 ×2 + [[Stick]] → Wand of Healing|
## Usage
Wand:
Wand of Healing
[Effects]
[Ability: Small Heal RIGHT CLICK; Heal 60 Health/s for 5s.; Wand heals don't stack.; Vitality Cost (Vitality): 25; Cooldown: 1s]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wand of Mending]]|Zombie Slayer III (3) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Wand of Healing]] 1x Wand of Healing 1x [[Revenant Viscera]] 12x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] + Wand of Healing + [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x12 → [[Wand of Mending]]|
## Tips
- The set bonuses of [[Revenant Armor]] and [[Reaper Armor]] increase healing from [[Wands|Healing Wands]] by 50%.
- Each armor piece enchanted with [[Habanero Tactics|Habanero Tactics V]] grants 7.5% additional healing from [[Wands]].
## Demonstration
Wand of Healing Demonstration
## Trivia
- Although consecutive casts do not stack, the first healing tick is applied immediately. With enough [[Vitality]], repeatedly casting the ability can heal 120 [[Health]] per second.

Warning Flare is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Deployables|Deployable]]. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer III (3)]] and can be upgraded into a [[Alert Flare]]. Healing effect only working on [[Crimson Isle]] and [[Kuudra]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer III (3) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|16x [[Enchanted Sulphur]] 16x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x16 + [[Molten Powder]] x8 ×2 → Warning Flare|
## Usage
Deployable:
Warning Flare
[Effects]
[Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK; Shoot the flare up in the sky for 3m buffing up to 8 players within 40 blocks.; Mana Cost: 300 Mana; Vitality Cost: 10 Vitality; Cooldown: 20s; Flare Buff: Warning Flare; Gain +10 Vitality.; Gain +10 True Defense.; Heal 30 Health per second while on the Crimson Isle and Kuudra.; Only one deployable buff applies.]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Alert Flare]]|Blaze Slayer VI (6) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Warning Flare]] 1x Warning Flare 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 32x [[Enchanted Sulphur]] 64x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x32 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Molten Powder]] x64 + Warning Flare → [[Alert Flare]]|
## Trivia
When placed, it can be removed by deploying other [[Deployables|Deployable]], waiting for [[Warning Flare]] Warning Flare to expire or going 80 blocks away.

The Water Snake Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Titanoboa Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Water Snake Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Water Snake]] with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].
### Huntraps
A Water Snake Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Spring Depths]] (Underwater) or [[Spring Shallows]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Water Snake Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Water Snake Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Titanoboa Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Titanoboa Tracker ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Tracking]] towards [[Titanoboa]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Weather Node
Categories: Uncommon items, Blocks, Craftable items, Pages with UIs, Prismarine Shard Collection, Tools, Other Tools
[Weather Node]
Type: Block
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Prismarine Shard VI (6) ([[Prismarine Shard]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 9x [[Lapis Lazuli]] 1,280x (20 stacks (1,280 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WEATHER_NODE
Tooltip: Change the weather on your private island.; UNCOMMON
The Weather Node is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] block that can be used to allow changing the weather on a [[Private Island]] through the [[Settings|Island Properties Settings]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Prismarine Shard VI (6) ([[Prismarine Shard]])|1x [[Block of Lapis Lazuli]] 8x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] ×8 + [[Block of Lapis Lazuli]] → Weather Node|
## Usage
When right clicked on [[Settings|Island Properties Settings]] menu, the Weather Node gets consumed and added to the features that allows to opens a menu to change the island's weather, which can be set to sunny, raining, or thundering. The selected weather will persist until another is chosen.
The [[Weather Wand]] can remotely access a placed Weather Node while its user is on their island.
## Chat Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Placing Weather Node|You placed a Weather Node! RIGHT CLICK it to control your island weather!|
|Selecting weather Sunny|You have changed the weather to: Sunny.|
|Selecting weather Raining|You have changed the weather to: Raining.|
|Selecting weather Thundering|You have changed the weather to: Thundering.|
|After selecting weather|Weather has been set in your Weather Node!|
|Trying to place more than one Weather Node|You can only place 1 of these on your island at a time!|
## Demonstration
Weather Node Demonstration
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Added Weather Node.|
|May 4, 2022|Weather Node's crafting recipe is no longer available from the [[Redstone Dust]] collection.|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Changed Weather Node from a placed block to a [[Settings]] feature.|

# Weather Wand
Aliases: Weather Stick

[Item Properties]

[Materials]

Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from merchants.): 1,280 [[Coins]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: Stick)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WEATHER_STICK
Tooltip: Ability: Weather RIGHT CLICK Allows you to change the weather on your island.; Cooldown: 5s; UNCOMMON WAND
The Weather Wand is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Wands|Wand]] that allows players to change the weather on their [[Private Island]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Redstone IV (4) ([[Redstone Dust]])|1x [[Stick]] 2x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] ×2 + [[Stick]] → Weather Wand|
## Usage
Item:
Weather Wand
[Effects]
[Ability: Weather RIGHT CLICK; Allows you to change the weather on your island.; Cooldown: 5s]
When right-clicked while on a [[Private Island]], the Weather Wand opens a menu to change the island's weather, which can be set to sunny, raining, or thundering. There's a 5-second cooldown between every use, and the weather will eventually change on its own.
If a [[Weather Node]] is placed on the island, the weather selected with the Weather Wand will be set in the node, letting it persist until another weather is chosen or the node is removed.
A Weather Wand can also be given to [[Professor Wynd]] to obtain their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Right-clicked while not on [[Private Island]]|You need to be on your island to use this!|
|Selecting weather Sunny|You have changed the weather to: Sunny.|
|Selecting weather Raining|You have changed the weather to: Raining.|
|Selecting weather Thundering|You have changed the weather to: Thundering.|
|After selecting weather with [[Weather Node]] placed|Weather has been set in your Weather Node!|
## Demonstration
Weather Wand Demonstration
## Trivia
- The Weather Wand's cooldown used to be bugged. Its ability description always displayed a cooldown time, but before version 0.19.1, there was no noticeable limit to how quickly a player could open its menu or select weathers.
- The Weather Wand used to be able to remotely pick up placed [[Weather Node|Weather Nodes]]. Attempting this without a node placed would display the message "That block isn't valid anymore!".

|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Renamed Weather Stick to Weather Wand.|

### Huntraps
A Wetwing Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Murkwater Depths]] (Underwater) or [[Murkwater Shallows]] (Underwater).

# Wishing Compass
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crystal Hollows
[Wishing Compass]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: True enough, this compass does not point north. It points to the thing you want most in this world.; The compass will prioritise important locations when in the Crystal Hollows
Source: Chests ([[Crystal Hollows]]) in the [[Crystal Hollows]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Crystal Hollows Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WISHING_COMPASS
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Points to important locations while in the Crystal Hollows.; Right-click to consume!; True enough, this compass does not point north. It points to the thing you want most in this world.; UNCOMMON
The Wishing Compass is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item which points players to important locations within the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
The player can find [[Wishing Compass]] 3 Wishing Compass in [[Crystal Hollows|treasure chests]] scattered around the Crystal Hollows.
## Usage
When the Wishing Compass is used, a line of green particles will appear. These particles make a straight line towards a certain location.
The Compass will point to different locations based on the conditions of the player.
- In [[Goblin Holdout]]:
  - Without [[Potions|King's Scent]]: Leads to [[King Yolkar]]'s location
  - With [[Potions|King's Scent]]: Leads to [[Goblin Queen's Den]]
- In [[Precursor Remnants]]: Leads to [[Lost Precursor City]]
- In [[Mithril Deposits]]: Leads to [[Mines of Divan]]
- In [[Jungle]]:
  - Without [[Jungle Key]] in inventory: Leads to [[Odawa]]'s location
  - With [[Jungle Key]] in inventory: Leads to [[Jungle Temple]]
- In [[Magma Fields]]: Leads to [[Khazad-dûm]]
- If all 5 [[Gemstone Crystals]] have been found and not yet placed, it will lead to [[Crystal Nucleus]]
Due to how [[Crystal Hollows]] lobbies generate, it is possible for some structures to not generate, leading to the Compass to say the following message instead, and the Compass does not get consumed:
The Wishing Compass can't seem to locate anything!
## Trivia
- There used to be a section of the lore that was a reference to Jack Sparrow's compass from the Pirates of the Caribbean.
  - The lore read: The origin of this unique compass is shrouded in mystery. It is said that it once belonged to the greatest pirates to sail the seas. The bigger mystery is how ended up so far underground.
## History

|Jul 20, 2021|Wishing Compass now prioritizes the most important rooms in your current biome first.|
|Jul 20, 2021|Wishing Compass will show its target direction for longer.|
|Jul 27, 2021|Improved Wishing Compass to target Crystals better.|
|Jul 27, 2021|No longer points to the Crystal Nucleus unless all Crystals have been collected.|
|Unknown Date|Removed section in lore about the origin of the Compass.|
|Aug 13, 2021|Wishing Compass can now be sold to Merchants ([[Shops]]).|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Wishing Compass added to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Wishing Compass now breaks after one use instead of 3.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|The Wishing Compass is now stackable.|

## Obtaining
### Sven Packmaster
Wolf Teeth can be obtained from any tier of [[Sven Packmaster]]:
- Tier I: [[Wolf Tooth]] 1–3 Wolf Tooth
- Tier II: [[Wolf Tooth]] 9–18 Wolf Tooth
- Tier III: [[Wolf Tooth]] 30–50 Wolf Tooth
- Tier IV: [[Wolf Tooth]] 50–64 (1 stack) Wolf Tooth
### Mob Drops

|[Lv109]|[[Pack Enforcer]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Red Claw Talisman]]|Wolf Slayer I (1) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x [[Enchanted Bone]] [[Wolf Tooth]] 8x Wolf Tooth|Recipe: Wolf Tooth x4 ×2 + [[Enchanted Bone]] → [[Red Claw Talisman]]|
|[[Golden Tooth]]|Wolf Slayer II (2) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|32x [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] [[Wolf Tooth]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Wolf Tooth|Recipe: Wolf Tooth x32 ×4 + [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] x32 → [[Golden Tooth]]|
|[[Radiant Power Orb]]|Wolf Slayer II (2) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|8x [[Enchanted Emerald]] [[Wolf Tooth]] 16x Wolf Tooth|Recipe: Wolf Tooth x2 ×8 + [[Enchanted Emerald]] x8 → [[Radiant Power Orb]]|
### Upgrading Pets
[[Wolf Tooth]] 512 (8 stacks) Wolf Teeth are required to upgrade [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Hound Pet]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].

# Wooden Bait
Aliases: Wood Bait, Wood bait
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Fishing
[Wooden Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WOODEN_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Ensures your next GOOD CATCH hooks a Rare Fish Shard.; Will not be consumed if no Rare Shards are available to catch.; UNCOMMON BAIT
Wooden Bait is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
Wooden Bait can be purchased from [[Rosemary]] for 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Forest Essence]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Wooden Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Wooden Bait will be consumed to guarantee that the next [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]] is a [[Attributes|Rare Fish Shard]]. Wooden Bait is not consumed if there are no [[Attributes|Rare Fish Shards]] to catch. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
Wooden Bait only works in these locations:
|Location|Overridden pools|Item rolled|Weight|Chance to override|Chance to override with Star Bait X ([[Attributes]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Crystal Hollows]]|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|[[Silentdepth Shard]]|0.5|100% (1 in 1 catches)|33.33% (1 in 3 catches)|
|[[Dwarven Mines]]|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|[[Abyssal Lanternfish Shard]]|0.5|100% (1 in 1 catches)|33.33% (1 in 3 catches)|
|[[Crimson Isle]]|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|[[Inferno Koi Shard]]|0.5|100% (1 in 1 catches)|33.33% (1 in 3 catches)|
|[[Moonglade Marsh]]|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|[[Piranha Shard]]|0.5|90.91% (1 in 1.1 catches)|32.26% (1 in 3.1 catches)|
|[[Moonglade Marsh]]|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|[[Eel Shard]]|0.05|9.09% (1 in 11 catches)|3.23% (1 in 31 catches)|
|[[Torrhus Canyon]]|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|[[Stingray Shard]]|7.14|100% (1 in 1 catches)|87.71% (1 in 1.14 catches)|
Since Wooden Bait removes all drops from the [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]] loot table except attribute shards, [[Shinyfish Shard]] becomes much more common:
|Location|Overridden pools|Item rolled|Weight|Chance to override with Star Bait X ([[Attributes]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Crystal Hollows]]; [[Dwarven Mines]]; [[Crimson Isle]]|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|[[Shinyfish Shard]]|1|66.67% (1 in 1.5 catches)|
|[[Moonglade Marsh]]|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|[[Shinyfish Shard]]|1|64.52% (1 in 1.55 catches)|
|[[Torrhus Canyon]]|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|[[Shinyfish Shard]]|1|12.29% (1 in 8.14 catches)|
## Notes
- The effects of Wooden Bait make [[Bouncy Beach Ball|Bouncy Beach Balls]] unobtainable while the bait is being used.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added Wooden Bait.|
|Jul 17, 2025|0.23.2 RC|Fixed an issue allowing players to fish up COMMON ([[Rarity]]) shards from a GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]]) while using Wooden Bait in the [[Tomb Floodway]].|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Wooden Bait as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Wooden Bait.|
|Jul 22, 2025|0.23.2|Fixed an issue allowing players to fish up COMMON ([[Rarity]]) shards from a GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]]) while using Wooden Bait in the [[Tomb Floodway]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Wooden Bait as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# Worm Bait
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Fishing
[Worm Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 25 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1x [[Worm Membrane]] 1x [[Clay Ball]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WORM_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +60 Fishing Speed but only Worms will bite.; Only works in the Crystal Hollows.; UNCOMMON BAIT
Worm Bait is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Bait]] that grants [[Fishing Speed]] and causes the player to only catch worm [[Sea Creatures]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Worm Membrane]] 1x [[Clay Ball]]|Recipe: [[Worm Membrane]] + [[Clay Ball]] → Worm Bait x16|
### Seal's Treat Bag
128x Worm Bait have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Worm Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Worm Bait will be consumed to grant +60 [[Fishing Speed]]. Note that the [[Fishing Speed]] doesn't show up in the player's stats. Additionally, the [[Player]] only catch worm type sea creatures in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
## Trivia
- When trying to use the bait outside of [[Crystal Hollows]] it is rejected and sends a message "You can't use this bait outside of Crystal Hollows." without consuming the bait.
- Before the 0.26 update, Worm Bait had the same texture as the [[Stoneworm]], despite being used to catch a completely different type of Worm.
- This item gives the most [[Fishing Speed]] of all the [[Bait|Baits]].
- While other creatures can be considered wormlike (such as the [[Sea Leech]] or the [[Lava Leech]]), this bait only works on the 3 Worm Sea Creatures in the [[Crystal Hollows]].

|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Worm Bait as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Damage: +5; Ability: Absorptio RIGHT CLICK This weapon lets you absorb 5% of the basic combat stats of any weapon.; Ability: Vis Temperata Each strike of this weapon has its damage capped at 15% of the enemy's max Health. This weapon cannot cause a fatal blow.; Ex tenebris, primus venator surgit.; This item can be reforged!; COMMON AXE
A Worn Huntaxe - Genesis is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Huntaxes|Huntaxe]].
## Obtaining
### Alan
A Worn Huntaxe - Genesis can be purchased from [[Alan]] for 25,000 [[Coins]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vinesap I (1) ([[Vinesap]])|2x [[Stretching Sticks]] 3x [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] ×3 + [[Stretching Sticks]] ×2 → Worn Huntaxe - Genesis|
## Usage
A Worn Huntaxe - Genesis has 2 abilities:
- Absorptio allows the Worn Huntaxe - Genesis to absorb 5% of the basic [[Stats|Combat Stats]] ([[Damage]], [[Strength]], and [[Crit Damage]]) of any weapon.
- Vis Temperata caps the [[Damage]] of the Worn Huntaxe - Genesis at 15% of the enemy's max health and prevents the user from killing the enemy. This allows one to easily syphon their [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] with a [[Small Pocket Black Hole|Small]] or [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus]]|Vinesap III (3) ([[Vinesap]])|[[Worn Huntaxe - Genesis]] 1x Worn Huntaxe - Genesis 8x [[Stretching Sticks]] 48x [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] x16 ×3 + Worn Huntaxe - Genesis + [[Stretching Sticks]] x8 → [[Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus]]|
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntaxe's description, "Ex tenebris, primus venator surgit." is Latin, and means "From darkness, the first hunter emerges."
  - The item's name, "Genesis", means "Beginning".
  - The ability name "Vis Temperata" means "Tempered force".
## History

|Jul 31, 2025|Added Worn Huntaxe - Genesis to Museum.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Worn Huntaxe - Genesis as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Added Worn Huntaxe - Genesis to Museum.|

The Yellow Belt is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Belts|Belt]]. It is obtainable from [[Master Tao]] after reaching 1000 points in the [[Dojo]]. It gives +15 [[Health]] and +5 [[Defense]]. Its ability reduces all damage taken by 1%. This item is automatically Soulbound upon receiving it. It can be upgraded into the [[Green Belt]], and is a direct upgrade from the [[White Belt]].

## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv93]|[[Yog]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|6%|
|[Lv263]|[[Bal]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|4.5%|
### Treasure Chests
Yoggie can be obtained from [[Treasure Chest|Treasure Chests]] in the [[Magma Fields]].

|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|

Item ID: YOUNGITE

Youngite is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] collection item. As an ore, it has the appearance of light blue stained glass pane.
## Collection
Collecting Youngite, [[Timite]], or [[Obsolite]] increases the player's [[Timite]] [[Collections|collection]], unlocking useful items and rewarding [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] at specific milestones.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|25|[[Time Gun]] Recipe; [[Eon Pickaxe]] Recipe|+4|
|II|50|Time Pocket Bag ([[Time Pocket]]) Upgrade|+4|
|III|100|[[Chrono Pickaxe]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|250|[[Highlite]] Recipe|+4|
|V|500|[[Satelite]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|750|[[Discrite]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
Youngite can be obtained by breaking its ore with an [[Anti-Sentient Pickaxe]] or its upgrades. Only 1 Youngite will drop.
- If replaced by a block of Air, it will take ?? [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Can't figure it out. Been staring at a block of air for 3 minutes and nothing has spawned. How does this work ?; When this info is added, remove this.) seconds to regenerate.
- If replaced by a block of [[Timite]] or [[Obsolite]], it will take 30 seconds to regenerate.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Discrite]]|Timite IV (4) ([[Timite]])|[[Youngite]] 64x Youngite 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Timite]] 256x [[Obsolite]]|Recipe: [[Obsolite]] x64 ×4 + [[Timite]] x64 ×4 + Youngite x64 → [[Discrite]]|
|[[Time Gun]]|Timite I (1) ([[Timite]])|1x [[Berberis Blowgun]] [[Youngite]] 32x Youngite|Recipe: Youngite x8 ×4 + [[Berberis Blowgun]] → [[Time Gun]]|
|[[Eon Pickaxe]]|Timite I (1) ([[Timite]])|1x [[Anti-Sentient Pickaxe]] [[Youngite]] 32x Youngite|Recipe: Youngite x8 ×4 + [[Anti-Sentient Pickaxe]] → [[Eon Pickaxe]]|
|[[Highlite]]|Timite IV (4) ([[Timite]])|16x [[Obsolite]] 32x [[Timite]] [[Youngite]] 32x Youngite|Recipe: Youngite x16 ×2 + [[Timite]] x16 ×2 + [[Obsolite]] x16 → [[Highlite]]|
|[[Satelite]]|Timite V (5) ([[Timite]])|1x [[Highlite]] 32x [[Obsolite]] 32x [[Timite]] [[Youngite]] 64x Youngite|Recipe: Youngite x16 ×4 + [[Timite]] x16 ×2 + [[Obsolite]] x16 ×2 + [[Highlite]] → [[Satelite]]|
### NPC Trading
[[Youngite]] 20 Youngite can be used to purchase [[Shen's Ringalia]] from [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X]].
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|
|-|-|-|
|[[Shen's Ringalia]]|Completion of Eleanor's Puzzle|25,000 [[Motes]] 64x [[Puff Crux]] [[Youngite]] 20x Youngite 20x [[Timite]] 20x [[Obsolite]] 1x [[Disinfestor Gloves]]|
### Timite
By using a [[Time Gun]], players can turn a Youngite ore into [[Timite]].

The Zealot Bruiser Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Ender Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Zealot Bruiser Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Zealot Bruiser]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Zealot Bruiser Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Zealot Bruiser Hideout]].
### Fusion
A Zealot Bruiser Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Zealot Bruiser Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Ender Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Ender Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against [[Ender]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Zealot Bruiser Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

||

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Zealot Bruiser Shard has the following description:
- These elite Zealots are fiercely loyal to the Ender Dragons, guarding their domains with unmatched strength.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Zealot Bruiser Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ZOMBIE_RING
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Reduces the damage taken from  Undead mobs by 10%.; ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 2.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Zombie Ring is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer II (2)]]. It reduces the damage taken from [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs by 10%.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Zombie Slayer LVL II (2) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Zombie Talisman]] 64x [[Revenant Flesh]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Flesh]] x32 ×2 + [[Zombie Talisman]] → Zombie Ring|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Zombie Artifact]]|Zombie Slayer LVL VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Zombie Ring]] 1x Zombie Ring 16x [[Enchanted Diamond]] 32x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 48x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Diamond]] x8 ×2 + [[Revenant Viscera]] x24 ×2 + Zombie Ring → [[Zombie Artifact]]|
## Tips
- It is extremely cheap for most players to buy the required [[Revenant Flesh]] from the [[Bazaar]], so they can upgrade the [[Zombie Talisman]] almost immediately after unlocking the recipe.
## Trivia
- The [[Zombie Artifact]], Zombie Ring, and [[Zombie Talisman]] used to stack to get 30% reduced damage taken from [[Zombie|Zombies]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Zombie Soldier Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Undead Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Undead Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Zombie Soldier Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Zombie Soldier Shard has the following description:
- Zombie Soldiers march tirelessly through the catacombs, their decayed bodies clad in ancient, rusting armor.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Zombie Soldier Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

# Combat Wisdom
Categories: Unknown Zone Referenced, Player Statistics
[Combat Wisdom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the Combat XP gain.
Increasing: Combat XP Boost Potion ([[Potions]]), [[Booster Cookie]], [[Pets]], Slayers ([[Slayer]]) and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☯
Unicode Code Point: U+262F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Book and Quill ()
Combat Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Combat|Combat XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Combat|Combat XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+CombatWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Combat Wisdom gained +100 [[Combat|Combat XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Combat|Combat XP]].
## Increasing Base Combat Wisdom
Players can permanently increase their Combat Wisdom by defeating unique tiers of [[Slayer]] bosses, up to 36. Additional 7 or 10 can be unlocked from [[Essence Shops]] and applied on specific islands.
### Slayers
Each unique tier of [[Slayer]] bosses grants Combat Wisdom. Tier I-Tier III grant 1 each and Tier IV and Tier V grant 2 Combat Wisdom each.
|Slayer|Tiers Tier I|Tiers Tier II|Tiers Tier III|Tiers Tier IV|Tiers Tier V|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Zombie Slayer]]|1|1|1|2|2|
|[[Spider Slayer]]|1|1|1|2|2|
|[[Wolf Slayer]]|1|1|1|2||
|[[Enderman Slayer]]|1|1|1|2||
|[[Vampire Slayer]]|1|1|1|2|2|
|[[Blaze Slayer]]|1|1|1|2||
### Essence Shops
|Essence Shop|NPC|Perk||||Notes|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Spider Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]])|[[Spider Tamer]]|Spider Trainer|3|5|10|Only while on [[Spider's Den]]|
|Dragon Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]])|[[Tyzzo]]|Ender Training|3|5|7|Only while on [[The End]]|
|Crimson Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]])|[[Vulcan]]|Crimson Training|3|5|10|Only while on [[Crimson Isle]] and [[Kuudra's Hollow]]|
- Defeating unique [[Slayer|Slayer Bosses]]. Each unique Tier I, II, and III boss defeated gives +1 Combat Wisdom, and each unique Tier IV and V boss gives +2 Combat Wisdom, up to a total of +36 Combat Wisdom.
- Obtaining the max level of Spider Training from the [[Spider Tamer]] ( 6,500 (101 stacks plus 36) [[Spider Essence]]), will grant you +10 Combat Wisdom while on the [[Spider's Den]].

## Maximizing Combat Wisdom
- [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Wolf Pet]] (+30 Combat Wisdom)
- [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Grandma Wolf Pet]] with a 20+ Kill Combo (+15 Combat Wisdom)
- In the Spider’s Den with [[Weather|Thunderstorm]] active (+10 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Booster Cookie]] (+25 Combat Wisdom)
- [[God Potion]] with a [[Celestial Mason Jar]] and [[Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin]] (+34 Combat Wisdom)
  - [[Potions|Combat XP Boost Potion III]] (+20 Combat Wisdom)
  - [[Viking's Tear]] (+10 Combat Wisdom)
  - [[Celestial Mason Jar]] (+3 [[Wisdom|Global Wisdom]])
  - [[Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin]] (+1 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Hunter Ring]] (+5 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Slayer]] (+36 Combat Wisdom)
  - [[Revenant Horror]] Tier V killed (+7 Combat Wisdom)
  - [[Tarantula Broodfather]] Tier V killed (+7 Combat Wisdom)
  - [[Sven Packmaster]] Tier IV killed (+5 Combat Wisdom)
  - [[Voidgloom Seraph]] Tier IV killed (+5 Combat Wisdom)
  - [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] Tier V killed (+7 Combat Wisdom)
  - [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier IV killed (+5 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Attributes|Veteran X]] (+10 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Essence Shops|Spider Essence Shop]] [Spider Training] while on [[Spider's Den]] (+10 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Blazetekk™ Ham Radio]] (+5 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Abicases|Blue™ but Red Abicase]] (+1.5 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Magic 8 Ball]] (+1 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Bubba Blister]] (+2 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Quantum|Quantum V]] on Weekends (+2 Combat Wisdom)
- [[Rift Necklace]] (+1 Combat Wisdom)
For a total of +187.5 Combat Wisdom
## Notes
- The following sources grant bonus [[Combat]] XP directly rather than Combat Wisdom:
  - Mayor [[Derpy]]
  - [[Champion]] and [[Toxophilite (Enchantment)|Toxophilite]]
  - [[Ghoul Pet]], [[Tarantula Pet]], [[Hound Pet]], [[Enderman Pet]], and [[Wisp Pet]]
  - [[Perma-Jelled Garlic-Flavored Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bear]]
  - [[Super Sharp 'n Stabby Steak Stake]]
  - Events granting Skill XP boost.
  - [[Champion]] and [[Toxophilite (Enchantment)|Toxophilite]] are additive with the bonuses from Pets, while everything else is multiplicative. [[Super Sharp 'n Stabby Steak Stake]] is applied before any multiplicative bonuses. [[Super Sharp 'n Stabby Steak Stake]] and Events granting Skill XP boost are multiplicative with each other.

# Mob
Aliases: Enemies, Enemy, Mob Categories, Mob Category, Mob Level, Mob Levels, Mob Lv, Mobs, Monsters
Categories: Unknown Zone Referenced, More info needed, Mobs, Mob Types, Minibosses, Bosses, Wolf Slayer, Enderman Slayer, Zombie Slayer, Spider Slayer, Fishing
[Mob]
Zombies ([[Zombie]]) spawn commonly on player islands.
Mobs are computer-controlled entities, which will usually attack players. Killing enough of a specific kind of mob can grant the [[Player]] levels for them in the [[Bestiary]].
[[SkyBlock]] has modified vanilla mobs to give them custom stats – [[Health]] and [[Damage]] – which determine their respective Mob Level, which will in turn scale the rewards for the player when defeated. Some higher tier enemies may have exclusive and useful drops. Additionally, Mobs are assigned 1 to 3 Mob Types and a Mob Category.

## Mob Levels
- [[Health]] and [[Damage]] determine Mob Levels.
- A Mob's Level can be read atop its head, at the beginning of its name tag (e.g. [Lv1] [[Graveyard Zombie]]).
- A Mob's Level determines the amounts of [[Skills|Skill XP]], [[Coins]] and [[Experience]] rewarded to the player when defeated.
### Formulas
For the overwhelming majority of Mobs, the following rewards are calculated depending on several factors.
|Mob Category|Mob Level FormulaMinimum = 1|Skill XP ([[Skills]]) FormulaMinimum = +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]]) or +25 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|[[Coins]] FormulaMinimum = 1 Coin ([[Coins]])| [[Experience]] FormulaMinimum = 1 Exp ([[Experience]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Mob|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.5⌋|⌊Mob Level/3⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.1⌋|
|[[Shielded]] Mob|⌊(log10(100,000×HP×DMG)−2)2.6⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.5⌋|⌊Mob Level/3⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.1⌋|
|Pest ([[Pest (Mob Type)]]) Mob|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6⌋|+1,500 Farming XP ([[Farming]])|⌊Mob Level/3⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.1⌋|
|[[Field Mouse]]|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6⌋|+6,000 Farming XP ([[Farming]])|⌊Mob Level/3⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.1⌋|
|MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6×1.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×7.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×2.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×3⌋|
|BOSS ([[Bosses]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6×1.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×7.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×2.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×3⌋|
|SLAYER MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6×1.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.5×TIER⌋|1|⌊Mob Level×3⌋|
|SLAYER BOSS ([[Slayer]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6×DIFF⌋|1|1|1|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6⌋|⌊Mob Level×7.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×0.75⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.1⌋|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6⌋|⌊Mob Level×10⌋|⌊Mob Level⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.2⌋|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6⌋|⌊Mob Level×12.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.25⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.3⌋|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6⌋|⌊Mob Level×15⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×1.5⌋|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6⌋|⌊Mob Level×20⌋|⌊Mob Level×2⌋|⌊Mob Level×2.5⌋|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])|⌊(log10(HP×DMG)−2)2.6×1.5⌋|⌊Mob Level×25⌋|⌊Mob Level×5⌋|⌊Mob Level×5⌋|
Remarks:
- TIER is the tier of Slayer Quest that the [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSS]] is eligible to spawn at.
- DIFF is the difficulty, manually set for [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] lacking a "proper" [[Damage]] stat. They are:
  - For [[Zombie Slayer]], DIFF=1.5
  - For [[Blaze Slayer]], DIFF=3.5
  As for the other [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSSES]], DIFF=1.5
calcname  = Mob Level, Coins, Skill EXP, Experience
 calcpage  = Calculator:Mob Level
 template  = Template:Calculator/Mob Level
 form      = MobLevelCalcForm
 result    = inner
 autoupdate= true
 
 
 param     = hp|Health||number|0
 param     = dmg|Damage||number|0
 param     = mob_category|Mob Category||select|Mob, Miniboss, Boss
 param     = shielded|Shielded Mob Type ?|false|select|true, false|mob_category == "Mob"
 param     = pest|Pest Mob Type ?|false|select|true, false|mob_category == "Mob"
 param     = field_mouse|Field Mouse ?|false|select|true, false|pest == "true"
 param     = slayer_mob|Slayer Boss or Miniboss ?|false|select|true, false|(mob_category == "Boss") or (mob_category == "Miniboss")
 param     = slayer_diff|Slayer Base Mob ?||select|Zombie, Spider, Wolf, Enderman, Vampire, Blaze|((mob_category == "Boss") and (slayer_mob == "true"))
 param     = slayer_tier|Slayer Quest Tier|4|int|1-5|((mob_category == "Miniboss") and (slayer_mob == "true"))
 param     = sea_creature|Sea Creature ?|false|select|true, false|mob_category == "Mob"

## Mob Types
Each mob has a specific [[Mob Types|type]], which usually dictate their characteristics. Some mob types have [[Enchantments]] and [[Attributes]] that are effective against their mob type.
|Type|Description|Effective Enchantment|Effective Attribute|Example|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Airborne]]|Enemies of this type can fly.|[[Gravity]]|Airborne Ruler ([[Attributes]])|Bat ([[Bat (disambiguation)]])|
|[[Animal]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in The Farming Islands ([[The Farming Islands (disambiguation)]]).||Animal Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Pig]]|
|[[Aquatic]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found when fishing ([[Sea Creatures]]).|[[Impaling]]|Aquatic Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Squid]]|
|[[Arcane]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in the [[Crimson Isle]].||Arcane Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Butterfly]]|
|[[Arthropod]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in the [[Spider's Den]].|[[Bane of Arthropods]]|Arthropod Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Dasher Spider]]|
|[[Construct]]|Enemies of this type are usually summoned by other enemies or players.||Construct Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Automaton]]|
|Critter ([[Critters]])|Enemies of this type are typically found on the islands of [[Galatea]] and must be defeated using [[Galatea]] tools.|||[[Coralot]]|
|Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]])|Enemies of this type are commonly found in the [[Deep Caverns]].|[[Cubism]]|Cubic Ruler ([[Attributes]])|Slime ([[Slime (disambiguation)]])|
|[[Elusive]]|Enemies of this type are rare and not seen very often.||Elusive Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Diamond Goblin]]|
|[[Ender]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in [[The End]].|[[Ender Slayer]]|Ender Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Zealot]]|
|Frozen ([[Frozen (Mob Type)]])|Enemies of this type are commonly found in [[Jerry's Workshop]].||Frozen Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Frozen Steve]]|
|Glacial ([[Glacial (Mob Type)]])|Enemies of this type are commonly found in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. Can only be damaged with a Pickaxe ([[Pickaxes]]) or Drill ([[Drills]]).|||[[Glacite Walker]]|
|[[Humanoid]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in the [[Crystal Hollows]].||Humanoid Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Grunt]]|
|Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])|Enemies of this type are commonly found in the [[Crimson Isle]].|[[Smoldering]]|Infernal Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Blaze]]|
|[[Magmatic]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in the [[Crimson Isle]].|[[Pyroclasm]]|Magmatic Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Fire Eel]]|
|[[Mythological]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found during the [[Mythological Ritual]].||Mythological Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Minos Hunter]]|
|Pest ([[Pest (Mob Type)]])|Enemies of this type are commonly found in [[The Garden]]. Can only be damaged with a vacuum.||Pest Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Mosquito]]|
|[[Shielded]]|Enemies of this type do not have conventional health and instead must be hit a certain number of times to kill them.|||[[Scatha]]|
|[[Skeletal]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]]).|[[Smite]]|Skeletal Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Skeleton]]|
|[[Spooky]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found during the Spooky Festival.|||[[Crazy Witch]]|
|[[Subterranean]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in the [[Dwarven Mines]].||Subterranean Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Treasure Hoarder]]|
|[[Timid]]|Enemies of this type will run away if see they see you approaching! Try your best to sneak up on them!|||[[Coralot]]|
|Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])|Enemies of this type are commonly found in the Hub ([[Hub Island]]).|[[Smite]]|Undead Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Zombie]]|
|[[Wither]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]]).|[[Smite]]||[[Crypt Dreadlord]]|
|[[Woodland]]|Enemies of this type are commonly found in [[Galatea]]. Can only be damaged with an Axe ([[Axes]]) or Huntaxe ([[Huntaxes]]).|[[Woodsplitter]]|Woodland Ruler ([[Attributes]])|[[Chillshot]]|
## Mob Categories
Certain Mobs fit into certain categories, which can affect their Level, [[Skills|Skill]] XP, and other non-item drops. These tags can be seen in [[Bestiary]] and above their nametags.
|Category|Description|Examples|
|-|-|-|
|MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])|[[Minibosses]] typically have high [[Health]] and [[Damage]].|[[Bladesoul]], [[Frozen Adventurer]]|
|BOSS ([[Bosses]])|[[Bosses]] are often found in specific spots of the world, and have high [[Health]] and [[Damage]].|[[Magma Boss]], [[Bonzo]]|
|SLAYER MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])|Slayer Minibosses ([[Minibosses]]) spawn while killing Mobs ([[Mob]]) needed to spawn slayer bosses.|[[Revenant Sycophant]]|
|SLAYER BOSS ([[Slayer]])|Slayer Bosses ([[Slayer]]) spawn while completing [[Slayer]] quests after gaining enough Combat XP ([[Combat]]) from killing certain mob types.|[[Revenant Horror]]|
### Sea Creatures
Sea Creatures have a chance to spawn when [[Fishing]]. This chance is a percentage, expressed as [[Sea Creature Chance]]. They drop unique fishing drops.
- [[Sea Creatures/List|Tab 1]]
### Slayer
[[Slayer|SLAYER BOSSES]] and [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSSES]] are exclusive to [[Slayer|Slayer Quests]].
- While active and incomplete, [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSSES]] may spawn: stronger than "normal" mobs, they award a larger amount of [[Combat|Combat XP]], significantly speeding up the completion of that specific [[Slayer]] quest.
- Upon completion, a [[Slayer|SLAYER BOSS]] appears. They tend to possess the following characteristics:
  - Even higher [[Health]];
  - [[Health]] regeneration when unable to damage players after about 15 seconds.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Isn't also if the player does not damage them?; When this info is added, remove this.)
  - Performs critical attacks when players are not moving, dragging them inwards and dealing high [[Damage Calculation|Melee Damage]] which gets stronger the more players are dragged.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; )
  - Teleports to the player if they are unable to reach the player.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Would be nice to know the criterion(a); When this info is added, remove this.)
## Trivia
- A [[Phoenix Pet]] can be obtained from killing any mob on [[Locations|Public Islands]]. A [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Phoenix Pet]] has a 0.000017% (1 in 6,000,000) chance to drop, and a [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] [[Phoenix Pet]] has a 0.000003% (1 in 30,000,000) chance to drop.
- Mobs that have 1B [[Health]] are the [[Kalhuiki Tribe Member]] and the [[Deathmite]]. Meanwhile, the [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Wither Dragons]] have 900M [[Health]] each.
- [[Derpy]]'s DOUBLE MOBS HP!!! perk doubles the [[Health]] of all Mobs.
- The [[Gold Mine]] is the only public island that does not have any Mobs.
- [[Slayer]] Bosses used to have a Mob Level formula based on their max [[Health]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 25, 2026|First AHN round featuring Mob Levels Improvements.|
|Sep 21, 2026|Second AHN round featuring Mob Levels Improvements.|
|Sep 21, 2026|Reduced [[Coins]] Formula (among other undocumented changes).|
|Sep 30, 2026|Third AHN round featuring Mob Levels Improvements.|
|Sep 30, 2026|Increased the [[Health]] conversion for [[Shielded]] mobs from 1,000 per 1 [[Health]] to 100,000 per 1 [[Health]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Mobs.|
|May 22, 2020|Mob kills no longer give Combat XP on private island.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Mob Levels are now based on the [[Health]] and [[Damage]] of the mob, rather than being set. They also now determine Skill ([[Skills]]) XP, [[Experience]] orb amounts, and [[Coins]] amounts.|

The Coins on Fire! is currently unobtainable.

- As the Coins on Fire! is a [[Mementos|Memento]], trading or auctioning it will cause the acquirer's name to be hidden, however the date and edition number will remain.
- Coins on Fire! is the only item in the game to have an exclamation mark in its name.
- This item was supposed to be obtained from Inflation Fixer [[City Projects|City Project]], but it was never released.

The Estranged Taikan Mask was an unobtainable [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] that would be obtainable from [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Special]].
## Obtaining
The Estranged Taikan Mask was originally intended to appear in [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Special]] while [[Diaz]] is elected with her Volume Trading perk, allowing for players to bid on it.

Estranged Taikan Mask [[Combat Wisdom]]: +5

## Trivia
- The helmet's texture resembles [[Maddox the Slayer]]'s head.
  - This is a reference to how both [[Maddox the Slayer]] and [[Aatrox]] were exiled from [[Scarleton]] due to their strength and lack of magic.
- The description of the Estranged Taikan Mask implies that the mask itself is estranged, rather than it being a mask of an estranged Taikan.

# Desecrated Shrine
Categories: Upcoming Features, Pages with broken file links, Locations
This article is about announced parts of SkyBlock that have not been released to the main server or the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]]. Its content is limited and prone to change.
[Desecrated Shrine]
Desecrated Shrine.png
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
The Desecrated Shrine is an upcoming instanced [[Ship|Fishing Island]], accessible via [[Ship|Ship Navigator]] once a currently unknown NPC is recruited into the player's [[Ship|Ship Crew]]. One of its notable features will be [[Guardian Slayer]].
## Features
### Instance
The Desecrated Shrine was said to be an instanced island, implying a similarity between it, [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] and [[Kuudra]].
### Guardian Slayer
The Desecrated Shrine will feature Guardian Slayer, the first fishing-based slayer boss. It will be spawned by slaying Desecrated Shrine's unique Sea Creatures and its abilities will be tied to the map of the island.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Announced that the next Slayer (whose intended release was stated to be in 2025) will be a fishing based one — [[Guardian Slayer]] — and that it will be placed on an instanced island.|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Revealed that since the island will be inherently tied to the slayer, it will feature some map-based mechanics.|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Revealed that Guardian slayer will be released after [[Lotus Atoll]], due it introducing a new system that the new island, and hence the slayer, will rely on.|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Revealed that Guardian Slayer is deep in development.|
|Dec 28, 2025|Revealed the name of the island tied to the Guardian Slayer to be Desecrated Shrine.|
|Dec 28, 2025|Planned release window for Desecrated Shrine and Guardian Slayer was stated to be in 2026.|
|Mar 23, 2026|Announced that the Desecrated Shrine will be the last Fishing update of 2026.|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Reaffirmed the release window of 2026.|
Linked from: [[Changelog]], [[Guardian Slayer]], [[Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki]], [[Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki/sandbox]], [[Locations]], [[Lotus Atoll]], [[Ship]]

# Pale Citadel
Aliases: Ashwood Citadel, Castle Ashwood
Categories: Upcoming Features, Locations, Unknown Zone Referenced
This article is about announced parts of SkyBlock that have not been released to the main server or the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]]. Its content is limited and prone to change.
[Pale Citadel]
Changelog-2026-04-28-castle ashwood.png Concept art
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
The Pale Citadel is an upcoming [[Combat]]-oriented island. Other than its name and planned release window, no features have been revealed: however, its gameplay is described as a 'raid'. It is intended to take full advantage of the latest Minecraft versions.
## Confirmed Features
- The Pale Citadel is described as a 'raid'.
- The Pale Citadel will have a reward system that directly rewards the player with [[Coins]].
- The Pale Citadel will feature a 'queueing' system instead of using [[Party Finder]].
For the Catacombs we currently don't have plans to change party finder. However for Pale Citadel we are doing a queueing system instead of party finder. You are still able to queue into it with a party if you have one, but if you are alone the system is a lot more streamlined. The content is designed around the fact that you might be playing with totally random people.
## Gallery
- Concept art for mobs intended to appear in the Pale Citadel
- The Marionette, a mob intended to appear in the Pale Citadel The [[Marionette]], a mob intended to appear in the Pale Citadel
- The Brute, a mob intended to appear in the Pale Citadel The [[Brute]], a mob intended to appear in the Pale Citadel
- The Battering Bull, a mob intended to appear in the Pale Citadel
## Trivia
- The Pale Citadel was initially referred to as Castle Ashwood, and then the Ashwood Citadel. It is the only area renamed twice.
- Some of the mobs in the Pale Citadel were made using tech originally intended for the minigame Ravengard.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 28, 2025|Announced plans to release "a new type of combat content" sometime in 2026.|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Concept art for the aforementioned content was shared, revealing the name Castle Ashwood.|
|Unknown Date|Announced the renaming of Castle Ashwood to Ashwood Citadel.|
|Jun 8, 2026|Revealed the intention for Ashwood Citadel to feature custom mobs.|
|Jun 8, 2026|Revealed that the Ashwood Citadel will likely be instanced, similar to the [[Catacombs]] or [[Glacite Mineshafts]].|
|Jun 8, 2026|55[ዞ] ([[Ranks]]) MotorGorilla describes the content as a Raid.|
|Sep 1, 2026|0.27.1|Announced the renaming of Ashwood Citadel to Pale Citadel.|
Linked from: [[Brute]], [[Changelog]], [[Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki]], [[Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki/sandbox]], [[Locations]], [[Marionette]]

# Changelog
Aliases: 0.6, History, Patch Notes, Patchnotes, Update, Updates, Version, Versions
Categories: Updates
[Changelog]
This page contains the history of past changes, bug fixes and updates to Hypixel SkyBlock, as posted on the Hypixel Forums by administrators and as discovered by the community.
Updates sources:
- News and Announcements
- Patch Notes
## Upcoming Updates
### Current Alpha
- 24/250 The Alpha Hypixel Network is currently open with a limit of 250 players.; When the data was last updated, there were 24 players online, so there is currently no queue.
### Future content
- [[Magic Find]] rework update that would reduce the need of swapping items to obtain maximum magic find, while also balancing the system out in general. [AHN] (This item or feature is currently only available on the Alpha Hypixel Network.)
- [[Accessory Bag]] revamp that will change it to a Museum-like menu that lists all obtained and missing accessories, making it easier to see which are missing as well as how to obtain them and if they can be upgraded.
  - This update is currently not planned to remove [[Jacobus]] and the accessory bag coin sink.
  - This update will also rework [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactors]] so they no longer use slots and are separated into one for each skill/sack.
- Early game Fishing improvements, improving the [[Fishing Outpost]] and tutorial and improving Fishing Bait crafting/obtainment.
- The [[Desecrated Shrine]], a new dedicated Fishing island, which will include [[Guardian Slayer]]. Still planned to release in 2026.
- The Great Glacite Lake update, with two pieces of BiS Mining Equipment, Glacite Sea Creatures, new Mineshafts, and an Offering system for the lake to target certain mineshafts.
  - This update may be related to the currently ongoing [[Dead Man's Hunt]].
- The Foraging Update, Part 3, which will include [[Lunarise]], the third and possibly final island of Galatea.
- New Combat content that is "intended to take full advantage of the latest versions of Minecraft" and planned to be "accessible to players at all stages of the game", including [[Pale Citadel]], and at least one new Slayer.
- Features and information confirmed in a Q&A session that took place on June 8th, 2026:
  - SkyBlock 1.0 will aim to use 1.21's features to its fullest
  - Custom prefix colors for [[SkyBlock Levels]] when functionality for custom colors is implemented.
  - A resource pack for menus, as seen in the 2024 Recap.
  - Concrete plans exist for the future of [[Alchemy]], which will not involve minigames similar to the [[Experimentation Table]].
### Not planned to be released soon
These features have been listed as upcoming or planned, but do not have any set release date or plan for release.
- [[Sea Creatures]] to drop rare Alchemy ingredients
- The dungeon shown on the left of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] (currently Not Coming Soon)
- [[Sven Packmaster]], [[Voidgloom Seraph]], and [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier V
- Dragon Rush and Primal Dragons (shelved due to performance issues, with no plans to revive it)
We had large technical issues with the update that would require major rewrites of certain parts of the game, so we don’t have any current plans on it. We might look into more end content in the future, but we currently do not have any plans on that either.
- [[Kuudra]] armor [[Crimson Essence]] cost reductions
We do still plan on doing the cost reductions, but it's not something that will urgently happen.
The reasoning behind nerfing the total Crimson Essence cost is twofold: the nerf will allow players to obtain a maxed Kuudra armor set in a reasonable amount of runs/gameplay, and there will be a world where Kuudra Armors aren't the strongest armor in the game, making the current grind not reasonable for how long it takes.
The cost reduction will indeed still happen in the future, along with a refund system that will grant you the difference. You should be safe to upgrade your Kuudra armors, but of course, Crimson Essence may become cheaper down the line (on the Bazaar) with a bunch of people getting refunds, so whether you upgrade your armors is up to you.
My apologies to not giving you an earlier update about it.
- [[SkyBlock 1.0]]
We still want to one day call SkyBlock "1.0", but we keep pushing the boundaries of what we have in mind for 1.0. Ultimately there is an update we have in mind, and when we will release this update, it’ll be called 1.0. But at this stage it would be a shame to release it without mastering all the latest versions features.

|PATCH|May 21|Protector QOL + Pet Balance + More|Added some new features to the [[End Stone Protector]]. Changed many pet stats and abilities. Added some more general QoL changes. Also fixed multiple bugs.|
|PATCH|May 19|Auction House Bin and Cancellation|Added Buy it Now (BIN) auctions to the game. Also, you may now cancel your auctions if they have 0 bids / haven't been purchased. Removed the NPC [[Warren]] from the game. Other general changes relating to the [[Auction House]].|
|PATCH|May 15|Item Buyback and Repelling Candle|Added the [[Repelling Candle]] to the game. Added the ability to buy back items that you sold to NPCs. Changed to the [[Bat Firework]] works.|
|PATCH|May 13|Fast Travel|Introduced the [[Fast Travel]] feature to the game. Added some other general miscellaneous changes including adding an extra round to the [[Dark Auction]].|
|0.7.8|May 5|Pets V2|Added [[Taming]]. Added 16 new [[Pets]] to the game. Added [[Pet Items]] and Pet Candy ([[Pet Candies]]). Added three new NPCs [[Kat]], [[Zog]], [[George]]. Did some balancing.|
|PATCH|April 3|New Bazaar Products|Added more products for trade to the [[Bazaar]].|
|PATCH|March 12|Dark Auction Upgrade|Changed the way the [[Dark Auction]] takes bids to prevent trolling. Dark Auction now has a queue and takes the richest players first. There are 330 player slots in the Dark Auction. Also fixed two bugs.|
|PATCH|March 11|Bazaar Tweaks|Added API support for the [[Bazaar]]. Added a toggleable chat notification for when an order is filled. Added confirmation when selling whole inventory, with a warning if it's below NPC sell price. Changed some icons in the menu. Added some new products to the Bazaar. Fixed and balanced some minions.|
|0.7.7|March 9|Bazaar|Added the [[Bazaar]] to the game. Also added 3 Fairy Souls to the hub and redecorated Bea's house.|
|PATCH|February 28|Plumber Joe & Bugs|Fixed many bugs. Added the NPC [[Plumber Joe]] and the [[Plumber's Sponge]] item. Also added Escrow to the game.|
|0.7.6|February 21|Pets and Quality of Life|Added 26 new pets to the game. Added the NPC Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper ([[Oringo]]). Added quick crafting to the game. Added 2 new Talisman Bag upgrades. Added the possibility to trade coins in the trading menu. Added many other QoL features. Added some balancing and some slayer related changes. Fixed many bugs.|
|PATCH|January 25|Bugfixes|Wounded potion can't be splashed on players anymore. Fixed Spooky event annihilating the network. A few other general changes.|
|PATCH|January 9|Stacking Patch|Small patch. Fixed "armor stacking" bug. Removed skeleton horse pet from players who exploited private island bed bug.|
|PATCH|January 8|Multiple Players Hitting Mobs|Enabled a functionality so that multiple players can hit the same monster at the same time. This feature works for all mobs in the game.|
### 2019
|Type|Date|Update|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|PATCH|December 22|Third Winter Event recap and changes|Fixed some issues with lag. More working on balancing mobs from the event, gifts rewarded, and rewards obtained from opening gifts. Added new items to the gift rewards, made the scoreboard show the timer until the next round, and buffed the Snow Suit ([[Snow Suit Armor]]) to give 2x stats on the Winter Island. Fixed multiple bugs.|
|PATCH|December 21|Winter Event changes|Made multiple things clearer and easier to understand. Changed the ratios of rewards. Increased server performance. Renamed the banker to "Banker Barry", as well as making some other general changes.|
|PATCH|December 19|First Winter Event recap and changes|Fixed multiple bugs and planned to fix many more. Also reduced the amount of players that can be on the Winer Island at once to make the Winter Event harder.|
|0.7.5|December 18|Winter Island & Balance Changes|Added Jerry's Workshop, also known as the Winter Island, on which the Winter Event happens. Added the variant of the hub village that appears during the Winter Event. Added new music for the event. Added 5 new tiers to the Ice Collection. A lot of balancing changes focused on armor and weapons. General changes include removing the possibility to open the profile of a player while holding a weapon, the cake bag now has 2x as much space, and fixed 2 issues that caused servers to crash.|
|PATCH|December 4|Piggy Bank Extras|The [[Piggy Bank]] now cracks first before creaking to reduce how often you have to repair the item. Reduced the cooldowns on the Personal Bank from (2hr, 1hr, 20m, no cd) to (1hr, 20m, 5m, no cd). Added a [[Personal Bank]] item which has the same functionality as the personal bank, except you can access it without going into the SkyBlock Menu.|
|PATCH|December 3|Bugfixes, Teleport Pad Renames, Pooch Sword, Piggy bank...|Fixed many bugs. Th [[Piggy Bank]] now protects you from losing coins upon death. Previous functionality of this item has been moved to the new feature, the [[Personal Bank]].|
|PATCH|November 18|Crafting, Dummy and Quiver Fixes|Fixed SkyBlock items being used in Minecraft recipes. Fixed other minor bugs. Romero now accepts any tier [[Flower Minion]]. Melody now sells the [[Personal Harp]].|
|0.7.4|November 11|SkyBlock Patch 0.7.4|Foraging island revamped to add more space for players. Added 26 new furnitures unlocked via [[Carpentry]]. Added 2 new sub-menus in the quest menu, one being the Fairy Soul menu to see how many Fairy Souls the player unlocked on each island, and a Completed Quests menu for completed quests. Added the NPC [[Walter]] to the Deep Caverns. Added a new shop made for tailoring to the village in the hub . Some small changes to how the Reaper Scythe works. Fixed some bugs. Other miscellaneous changes including the removal of extra Jerries ([[Jerry]]) on your island.|
|PATCH|November 6|Spooky Event rewards changes|The top 1 reward in the [[Spooky Festival]] now only requires to finish the event with at least 2400 Points. The top 10 reward now only requires to finish the event with at least 2000 Points. Other rewards are unchanged.|
|0.7.3|October 24|SkyBlock Patch 0.7.3|Added the [[Spooky Festival]]. Improved the events menu by allowing you to claim items you forgot to claim from previous events. Added the [[New Year Cake Bag]] to hold new year cakes and give them a purpose other than just being a collectible. New items in the Pumpkin collection. Reworked how Zealots and Special Zealots work. Changed some mechanics in the [[Colosseum]]. Added some functionality to the bartender by allowing him to sell brews. Added 3 new songs to the game. Other general changes include enchanted items can no longer be used to craft vanilla recipes, Watchers are now correctly dropping arrows, and the Intimidation Talisman now works with Spiders and Blazes.|
|PATCH|October 17|Damage system fixes|A few bugfixes related to the damage system. Abilities and enchants no longer apply invulnerablility ticks on mobs, such as Pigman Sword's ([[Pigman Sword]]) Burning Souls and the [[Thorns]] enchantment. Fixed Extreme Focus on [[End Stone Sword]] so the resistance bonus doesn't go away on the first hit and added some sfx to it. There were also some changes to the slayer system including normalizing the Slayer XP required for Broodfather & Packmaster LVLs so they're the same as Zombie. Also reduced the damage of dark-red mini-bosses by a factor of 2 and added a minute to Sven's time limit.|
|PATCH|October 15|More Slayer Changes|Revenant/Tarantula armor now flash the player's bonused defense when hit by the zombies/spiders. Added chat message + SFX when getting a rare slayer drop from bosses. Sven Packmaster now dramatically reduces bow damage and will now slamdunk its target from afar if they haven't moved in a bit. Shifted some items in the Zombie Slayer progression. Added more spiders to the upper levels of the Spider's Den. Reduced Maddox's Batphone cooldown from 5 to 3 minutes. Made mini-bosses spawn more consistently during slayer quests. Fixed Revenant/Tarantula armor not properly giving their Bulwark effect and then temporarily disabled the fix.|
|PATCH|October 14|Small Slayer Fixes|Many Slayer related fixes including boss balancing, slayer gear working incorrectly, and a few fixes on other slayer related things. Also fixed fishing rod bobbers not applying the rod's damage, a bug with infinite quiver, and some translation errors.|
|0.7.2|October 11|SkyBlock Patch 0.7.2|Added Slayers ([[Slayer]]) and Slayer Quests, along with Maddox under the Tavern. Many minions were balanced. Also added Bank Upgrades and the Skyblock API.|
|PATCH|September 17|Default profiles can now become co-ops|Solo default profiles can now be able to invite members. Also the Skill leaderboards are now fixed, and will correctly update.|
|0.7.1|September 11|SkyBlock Patch 0.7.1|Over 100 bug fixes. Backend projects including performance improvements. New items including the [[Sculptor's Axe]], Greater Backpacks ([[Backpack]]), and Colored Backpacks ([[Backpack]]). Added more QoL recipes. The Talisman bag renamed to Accessory Bag. Having multiple of the same accessories in your inventory won’t count the extra reforge stats. Items that can be reforged now have a text at the bottom. New collection rewards. Dragons ([[Ender Dragon]]) are now more tanky and servers will now delay shutdown until a dragon fight is over. Super Compactors now work with wool and with fishing and flower minions. New balancing changes for minions. Changed the auction displays to only show the hottest auctions, instead of the highest prices. The bank deposit element in the bank now shows the time until next interest. You may now purchase a Hub Portal from Jerry. Improved the way coin gains and skill exp gains are displayed on the screen. Other miscellaneous changes.|
|PATCH|September 10|Profiles Update|An update to the SkyBlock profiles system. Mostly focuses on the backend systems, but there's a few new features. New Co-op Management menu which includes a new co-op kick feature. In order to keep track of players who transited through the profiles, there's a new "Historical Members" button. Within the historical members you can see extra info about their history, like when they left and why.|
|PATCH|September 5|Corrupted Worlds Fix|Fixed a rare bug which prevented some players from joining SkyBlock.|
|PATCH|August 15|Changelog|Many bug fixes. True Damage from some dragon abilities increased. NPC sell price of [[Frost Walker]] decreased. Added a drop message when you get a drop that has a 1% or lower chance of dropping. Added 2 new QoL recipes, for [[Enchanted String]] and for [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]].|
|0.7|August 2|The End, Carpentry, Runes, and more!|[[The End]] added. [[Perfect Armor]], [[Emerald Blade]], [[Rusty]] NPC, daily shop limit of 128 and [[Magical Water Bucket]] added. "Claim all" button and scam precautions added to the [[Auction House]]. Fuels ([[Minion Fuel]]) now use real-time instead of SkyBlock time. Leaping ([[Leaping Sword]]), [[Pigman]] and [[Golem Sword]] abilities buffed. HP of [[Bosses]] tweaked. [[Bows]] deal less damage and cannot crit if they aren't fully drawn. Ambient Cave by ShinkoNet added to [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]]. Many tweaks, as well as bug and exploit fixes.|
|PATCH|July 21|Changelog|Bug and exploit fixes.|
|PATCH|July 19|Changelog|Fixed a dupe exploit, inventories not saving, being able to break blocks in the [[Hub Island]], as well as adding performance improvements.|
|PATCH|July 15|Changelog|Bug and exploit fixes.|
|PATCH|July 12|Changelog|Bug fixes, including a dupe exploit involving [[Minion Storage]].|
|PATCH|July 11|Changelog|Fixed Telekinesis ([[Telekinesis (Enchantment)]]) working on [[Bosses]] and not working on [[Bows]]. Fixed item stacking issues with [[Silk Touch]]|
|0.6|July 9|Guesting, Teleport Pads, and more!|You can now visit other players' islands ([[Private Island]]). New Boss ([[Bosses]]) loot weight system added, first implementation of client-side loot rather than bosses dropping loot. [[Hot Potato Book]], 11 new [[Achievements]], Collection ([[Collections]]) and Skill ([[Skills]]) leaderboards added, among others. You can now bulk buy from NPC shops. Enchants nerfed in damage formula.|
|PATCH|July 8|Changelog|Many bug fixes.|
|0.5|July 2|Fishing, Achievements and more!|[[Fishing]] overhaul adding [[Sea Creatures]] and increasing Fishing XP gain by 4 times. 96 new [[Achievements]] added. Skill levels ([[Skills]]) 26-50, [[Redstone Meter]] and [[Dark Auction]] added. The Guide is now [[Jerry]].|
|PATCH|June 29|Changelog|[[Protection]] enchant buffed from 2 [[Defense]] to 3 [[Defense]] per level. Nerfed cactus sell price from 3 [[Coins]] to 1 Coin ([[Coins]]). Enchanted items can now be crafted without arranging them in their specific recipe shape.|
|0.4|June 26|Changelog|Deliveries ([[Lost & Found]]) added to The Guide ([[Jerry]]). Added [[Magical Mushroom Soup]], Jungle Axe ([[Spruce Axe]]), Day ([[Day Saver]]) and [[Night Saver]], among others. Many balancing changes, tweaks and bug fixes.|
|0.3|June 20|Armor and Weapons Balancing Changes|Many armor sets, weapons and enchantments tweaked including Growth V ([[Growth]]) enchant nerfed from 100 [[Health]] to 75 [[Health]] . Several bug fixes and tweaks.|
|PATCH|June 20|Changelog|Several bug fixes.|
|PATCH|June 17|Changelog|Several bug fixes and tweaks. Including fixing New Year Cakes ([[New Year Cake]]) being claimable more than once.|
|0.2|June 13|Changelog|SkyBlock Prototype opened to all players. Lowered amount of crafts needed for new minion slots. Added COMMON ([[Rarity]]) rarity tag. Several bug fixes.|
|0.1|June 11|New Prototype Game - Hypixel SkyBlock|SkyBlock Prototype opened to ranked players|

# Changelog/2026/July 22/Announcement
Categories: Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
Follow-up Message on Healing Revamp Alpha Testing
Updated: July 22, 2026
Hello! Thank you for your feedback throughout the various testing sessions of Healing Revamp. I hope that the explanations and adjustments regarding Healing Revamp have hit the mark, and that the balancing of the update is adequate for a release.
Your feedback has been very helpful to us, we couldn’t have released the update without you.
We are still looking to make balancing adjustments to ensure that gameplay is in a good state.
We have previously hinted at/are considering the following changes and would like further feedback before going forth with them:
- Giving some Vitality to Goldor and Hollow Armor.
  - While we’re not against giving those armor sets some Vitality, it’s worth noting that those sets naturally synergize with healing because they’re inherently tankier. A heal worth, say, 500 HP is worth more when you have 5,000 defense opposed to 1,000 defense.
  - In reverse, we’re also plenty aware that players in “tank” armor will naturally take on roles that require taking more damage, and need more healing as a result.
  - Let us know what specific situations you think this additional Vitality is crucial for, and we can determine how much Vitality you need accordingly.
- Healing on Powerflux and Flares
  - We’ve mentioned on the previous design thread that we’re open to Flares giving healing globally, as long as it does not infringe on the design space of Powerflux as a healing deployable.
    - That is the justification for the current design, but it doesn’t mean we can’t adjust the concept.
  - With this prospective change, Flares will:
    - Heal for half the amount (that it currently heals for Crimson Isle and Kuudra) on all other islands.
      - 30/40/50 in CI, halved to 15/20/25 on other islands.
    - The Vitality Cost is increased from 10 to 50.
  - Let us know what you think about this change, or perhaps, you may have a better suggestion!
- Vampire Dentist Relic
  - As mentioned previously, we’re looking to let the natural healing tick proc more frequently at a smaller number.
- Life Steal V
  - We are aware that Life Steal V isn’t feasibly obtainable outside of the Experimentation Table. While we don’t believe Life Steal V is necessary for general content completion, we will look into ways to let Life Steal V be more obtainable while keeping it valuable.
    - We’re considering adding it into the loot table of Vampire Slayer 4 and 5.
- Berserk’s Healing on Kill change
  - We initially made the change because healing on hit is a very strong mechanic, so we wanted to restrict the healing a bit. We’re still open to adjusting the amount of healing that comes from this ability.
- Berserk Melee Damage Bonus
  - Berserk’s Melee Damage Bonus is currently additive, that is admittedly very weak, we’re looking to make it multiplicative.
- Lava Bouncing in 26.1
  - We’re aware that Lava Bouncing in versions 26.1 and higher does not work the same way as in previous versions. We’ll look into this issue as soon as possible.
- Vanquishers and other sources dealing True Knockback
  - We’re aware that some mobs, such as Vanquishers, are dealing knockback that does not respect knockback immunity. We’re working on a fix and would appreciate reports on which other mobs this bug is affecting.

# Bags
Aliases: Bag
Categories: Upgrades, Mechanics
Bags are storage features that can store items, generally unlocked through different [[Collections]]. They can be accessed through the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
## List of Bags
|Bag|Description|Source|Slots|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Potion Bag]]|A handy bag for holding your [[Potions]] and [[Mixins]] in.|Nether Wart II (2) ([[Nether Wart]]) onward|9 -> 18 -> 27 -> 36 -> 45; Nether Wart II / V / VIII / X / XII|
|[[Accessory Bag]]|A special bag which can hold Talismans, Rings, Artifacts and Orbs within it. All will still work while in this bag!|Redstone Dust II (2) ([[Redstone Dust]]) onward, [[Elizabeth]], [[Jacobus]], [[Redstone Miner]]|9 by default, up to 281 total; 48 from Redstone Dust, 4 from Redstone Miner, 12 from Elizabeth, 198 from Jacobus, 10 from the Accessory Size ([[Attributes]]) attribute|
|[[Personal Bank]]|Allows access to the [[Bank]] from anywhere.|Emerald VI (6) ([[Emerald]]), upgraded via [[Guy]]|No slots; Cooldown: 1h -> 20m -> 5m -> none|
|[[Ender Chest]]|Store global items that you want to access at any time from anywhere here. Unlike most upgrades, you start off with a non-upgraded version of this in the menu.|[[Elizabeth]]||
|[[Quiver]]|A masterfully crafted Quiver which holds any kind of projectile you can think of!|String III (3) ([[String]]) onward|27 -> 36 -> 45; String III / VI / IX|
|[[Sack of Sacks]]|A sack which contains other sacks. Sackception!|Tropical Fish IV (4) ([[Tropical Fish]]) onward, plus [[Account & Profile Upgrades]]|8 -> 12 -> 16 -> 20 -> 24 -> 29, plus up to 16 from Account Upgrades; Tropical Fish IV / V / VI / VII / VIII / IX|
|[[Time Pocket]]|A bag which holds items that evolve over time. Time flows twice as fast in here!|Timite II (2) ([[Timite]])||
Upgrade tiers no longer use names such as Small or Medium for the Potion Bag, so the table lists slot counts instead.
