# Absorption
Categories: Vanilla objects, Player Statistics, Health, Mechanics
Absorption is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Absorption.
[Absorption]
Type: Stat
Uses: Protection from losing [[Health]]
Increasing: Absorption ([[Absorption Potion]]) and [[Adrenaline Potion]], [[Dctr. Paper]], Wither Shield ([[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]) and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E010
Absorption is a mechanic present in vanilla Minecraft. It is expanded upon in SkyBlock.
It can be thought of as an extension of the player's [[Health]] and is affected by [[Defense]]. When damage is inflicted to Absorption, it is removed. Unlike [[Health]], Absorption will not regenerate over time after taking damage.
## Increasing Bonus Absorption
### Armor
Certain [[Armor]] pieces grant Absorption. The [[Armor]] must be equipped to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Golem Armor]]|[[Golem Armor]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Helmet ([[Helmets]])|+60 for 20 seconds after killing a mob|
|[[Golem Armor]]|[[Golem Armor]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Chestplate ([[Chestplates]])|+60 for 20 seconds after killing a mob|
|[[Golem Armor]]|[[Golem Armor]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[[Leggings]]|+60 for 20 seconds after killing a mob|
|[[Golem Armor]]|[[Golem Armor]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[[Boots]]|+60 for 20 seconds after killing a mob|
### Sword Upgrades
Certain [[Swords|Sword Upgrades]] grant Absorption. The [[Swords|Sword Upgrade]] must be applied to a sword in order to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]|Wither Shield ([[Necron's Blade Scrolls]])|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Necron's Blade Scroll ([[Necron's Blade Scrolls]])|984-1400|
|[[Necron's Blade Scrolls]]|Wither Impact ([[Necron's Blade Scrolls]])|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Necron's Blade Scroll ([[Necron's Blade Scrolls]])|984-1400|
### Brews
Certain [[Brews]] grant Absorption. The [[Brews|Brew]] must applied to a potion in order for it to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dctr. Paper]]|[[Dctr. Paper]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Brew ([[Brews]])|+75|
### Potions
Certain [[Potions]] grant Absorption. The [[Potions|Potion]] must be drunk in order for it to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Absorption Potion]]|[[Absorption Potion]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) - EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Potion ([[Potions]])|+20-300 for 3 minutes|
|[[Adrenaline Potion]]|[[Adrenaline Potion]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) - EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Potion ([[Potions]])|+20-300 for 3 minutes|
### Foodstuffs
Certain Foodstuffs grant Absorption. The Foodstuff must be eaten in order for it to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type|[[Mending]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Golden Apple]]|[[Golden Apple]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Foodstuff|+20 for 2 minutes|
|[[Enchanted Golden Apple]]|[[Enchanted Golden Apple]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Foodstuff|+80 for 2 minutes|
### Miscellaneous
|Slot|Item|Type|[[Mending]]|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dungeon Orb]]|Wish ([[Healer]])|Class Passive ([[Classes]])|+20-70%|
## Trivia
- You can regenerate Absorption if you take damage from an [[Obsidian Defender]] and get the wither debuff.
- Absorption potions cannot be used to absorb damage from the [[Trials of Fire]].
## History

|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Absorption.|

# Acacia Log
Aliases: Acacia, Acacia Wood
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Blocks, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Acacia Log Collection, Collections, Wood
Acacia Log is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Acacia Log.
[Acacia Log]
Type: Wood
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Block Details]
Tool ([[Tools]]): Axe ([[Axes]])
Skill ([[Skills]]) XP Given: +6 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]])

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

Acacia Log is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Foraging]] [[Collections|Collection]] item.
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Acacia Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|Acacia Leaves ([[Leaves]]) Trade|+4|
|III|250|[[Enchanted Acacia Log]] Recipe; Portal to Savanna Woodland ([[Portals]]) Recipe|+4|
|IV|500|[[Savanna Bow]] Recipe|+4|
|V|1,000|Savanna Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]]) Recipe|+4|
|VI|2,000|+2,000 [[Foraging]]Experience|+4|
|VII|5,000|[[Repelling Candle]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|10,000|+10,000 [[Foraging]]Experience|+4|
|IX|25,000|[[Acacia Birdhouse]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
It is obtainable by breaking down Acacia Logs in the [[Savanna Woodland]] in [[The Park]] and from [[Acacia Minion|Acacia Minions]]. Breaking Acacia Logs grants +6 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]]. It is a collectible under the foraging section.
### Merchants
|Merchant|Price ([[Coins]]) 1x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 5x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 10x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 32x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 64x|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lumber Merchant]]|5 [[Coins]]​|25 [[Coins]]​|50 [[Coins]]​|160 [[Coins]]​|320 [[Coins]]|
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv51]|[[Howling Spirit]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|15%|
## Usage
Acacia Log can be used as a basic building block on the [[Private Island|Player's Island]] and for some basic crafting. It is also used for crafting [[Acacia Minion|Acacia Minions]] and can be sold at a [[Shops|Merchant]] for 2 [[Coins]] per block.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Portal to Savanna Woodland ([[Portals]])|Acacia Log III (3)|16x [[Ender Pearl]] [[Acacia Log]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Acacia Log|Recipe: Acacia Log x16 ×8 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|
|Acacia Minion I ([[Acacia Minion]])|Acacia Log I (1)|1x [[Wooden Axe]] [[Acacia Log]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Acacia Log|Recipe: Acacia Log x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Axe]] → [[Acacia Minion]]|
|Acacia Minion II ([[Acacia Minion]])|Acacia Log I (1)|1x Acacia Minion I ([[Acacia Minion]]) [[Acacia Log]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Acacia Log|Recipe: Acacia Log x20 ×8 + [[Acacia Minion]] → [[Acacia Minion]]|
|Acacia Minion III ([[Acacia Minion]])|Acacia Log I (1)|1x Acacia Minion II ([[Acacia Minion]]) [[Acacia Log]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Acacia Log|Recipe: Acacia Log x40 ×8 + [[Acacia Minion]] → [[Acacia Minion]]|
|Acacia Minion IV ([[Acacia Minion]])|Acacia Log I (1)|1x Acacia Minion III ([[Acacia Minion]]) [[Acacia Log]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Acacia Log|Recipe: Acacia Log x64 ×8 + [[Acacia Minion]] → [[Acacia Minion]]|
|[[Enchanted Charcoal]]|Coal V (5) ([[Coal]])|32 Log ([[Wood (disambiguation)]]) (Vanilla) 128 (2 stacks) [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] x32 ×6 + [[Oak Log]] x32/[[Birch Log]] x32/[[Spruce Log]] x32/[[Dark Oak Log]] x32/Acacia Log x32/[[Jungle Log]] x32 → [[Enchanted Charcoal]]|
|Acacia Planks ([[Planks]])|None|[[Acacia Log]] 1x Acacia Log|Recipe: Acacia Log → [[Planks]] x4|

Anvil is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Anvil.

[Item Properties]

Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 45 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ANVIL
Tooltip: COMMON
Anvil is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block.
## Obtaining
### Locations
|NPC|Location|
|-|-|
|[[Blacksmith]]|[[Village]]|
|[[Blacksmith]]|[[Gold Mine]]|
|[[Blacksmith]]|[[Dwarven Village]]|
|[[Alwin]]|[[Scarleton]]|
|[[Igor]]|[[Dragontail Blacksmith]]|
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|3x [[Block of Iron]] 4x [[Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Block of Iron]] ×3 + [[Iron Ingot]] ×4 → Anvil|
## UI
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Usage
### Combining Enchanted Books
An Anvil can be used to combine [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] to increase their level, as in Vanilla Minecraft. it costs no additional [[Experience]] levels to do so, and as a result, gives no [[Enchanting]] experience. This does not work for all levels, however, as many high-level enchantments (usually level VI or higher) are obtained from [[Dungeons]], [[Experimentation Table|Experiments]], or the [[Dark Auction]].
### Item Modifiers
An Anvil can be used to apply modifiers like [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]], [[Endcaps|Endcap Enchantments]], [[Hot Potato Book]] or [[Book of Stats]] to an item.
### Combining Tools
Two items of the same type can be combined to add their Enchantments. The item on the right slot will be deleted.
### Applying Reforge Stones
To apply a [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]], place it on the right side of the Anvil and the item to reforge on the left side. Most Reforge Stones have a [[Mining]] skill requirement and a cost in [[Coins]] to apply.
### Cosmetics
Anvil also can be used to apply [[Backpack Skins]], [[Deployable Skins|Power Orb Skins]], [[Helmet Skins]], [[Dyes|Custom Dyes]] and use [[Ditto Blob]].
## Trivia
- In SkyBlock, players cannot damage an Anvil by simply using it. The only known way of damaging an anvil in SkyBlock is by dropping it from a height larger than 1 block. The chance of the anvil getting damaged is 5% per block fallen. When an anvil gets damaged for the 1st time, it will turn into a Chipped Anvil. Damaging it again will turn it into a Damaged Anvil. Damaging it another time breaks it.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Anvil.|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|[[Reforge Anvil]] has its functionality replaced by Anvils.|

# Awkward Potion
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Potions, Brewing, Alchemy
Awkward Potion is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Awkward Potion.
[Awkward Potion]
Type: Brewing item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Use Awkward Potions in Brewing Stand with items labeled as Brewing Ingredients to brew Potions.
Uses: Brewing
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1x [[Water Bottle]] 1x [[Nether Wart]]
Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from merchants.): 16–28 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POTION
Tooltip: Use Awkward Potions in Brewing Stands with items labeled as Brewing Ingredients to brew Potions.; COMMON
Note: If you are having issues placing Water Bottles in a brewing stand, they are probably [[Glitched Items|Tagless Items]]. Throw them on the ground and pick them up to fix the issue.
Awkward Potions are the most common [[Potions|Brews]], useless on their own but used to make any brewable [[Potions|Potion]] except for [[Potions|weakness potion]]
Certain potions can have the awkward potion replaced by one of the other Brews, which can be bought from the [[Bartender]], [[Shifty]] and [[Melancholic Viking]] for added stat bonuses. It is a universal [[Alchemy|Base Potion]].
## Brewing
It is made by adding [[Nether Wart]] to [[Water Bottle|Water Bottles]].
## Experience
As a part of the features specific to Hypixel SkyBlock, brewing potions was turned into a skill, [[Alchemy]].
Crafting one Awkward Potion gives +5 [[Alchemy]] Experience, and a player can make three at a time using only one [[Nether Wart]].
That makes the cost efficiency of making Awkward Potions for [[Alchemy]] experience approximately 0.31 exp/coin if making one at a time, or 0.54 exp/coin if making three at a time.

## Vanilla Axes
Axes is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Axes.
All vanilla Minecraft tools exist in SkyBlock, although SkyBlock alternatives are almost always better. Since tools don't degrade on use in SkyBlock, a common myth is that diamond axes are the fastest vanilla axe, but Golden Tools are actually the best due to their speed. The wooden version is used in various [[Minions|Minion]] recipes.

## Trivia
- There used to be [[Swords]] which took the shape of Axes ( [[Raider Sword|Raider Axe]], [[Ragnarock|Ragnarock Axe]], [[Daedalus Blade|Daedalus Axe]], [[Halberd of the Shredded|Axe of the Shredded]], [[Mercenary Sword|Mercenary Axe]], and [[Enrager]]), however they always had the type of "Sword" and couldn't have any Axe enchantments. All of those items were turned into Swords in the 0.23 Update.
- There used to be [[Farming Tools]] which appeared as Axes ( [[Pumpkin Dicer]], [[Melon Dicer]], and [[Cocoa Chopper]]). They were converted into Farming Tools with the introduction of that item type in the 0.24.4 Update.
- Although the previous version of [[Habanero Tactics]] had "Axes" in its description, it in fact did not work on tool axes but on [[Sword Types|Axe-Type]] [[Swords]].
- Even though Axes aren't used for combat, for a time [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; until when?; When this info is added, remove this.) they could have had [[Vicious]] applied onto them.

# Banner
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Blacklisted Items
Banner is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Banner.
[Banner]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WALL_BANNER
Tooltip: 
The Banner is an [[Blacklisted Items|outdated]] item that was previously obtained using bugs and exploits. Normal banners are not craftable. It disappears from the inventory when traveling servers and interacting with it in menus. It has an interesting effect upon how the game renders in certain versions.
## Properties
Banners were obtainable through 2 main methods. The first one was breaking the banners in the Hub with the [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]] enchant while in an unprotected [[Dark Auction]] lobby. The second method was when the [[Traveling Zoo]] was first introduced, which cause the stands to spawn on player's islands, allowing them to break the banners.
Because the type of banner you were able to obtain was specific to post-flattening (1.13+) versions (WALL_BANNER), 1.8 couldn't render them and would crash if seeing a banner inside a GUI, or have interesting results when another player held it.The texture for these items in 1.8 clients' GUIs was later changed to a stone block, but holding it would still reveal a banner to other players. Due to some glitches, banners became reasonably common, though they later became blacklisted to prevent game crashes. This makes them incredibly hard to transfer, and they will delete upon changing servers.
Banners disappear when interacted with in many different menus, so one of the best ways to ensure they don't get deleted is to place them.
## Trivia
- An unknown update allowed for banner ids that were previously invisible in versions of 1.8 to become visible. It is unknown why these changes came into effect.
- TimeDeo's video showed the NBT data of one of the banners at the end of his "how i lost 800,000,000 coins" video. As every other item in SkyBlock, the data includes ExtraAttributes:{id:"WALL_BANNER"}, which classifies the item as wall banner (which, in vanilla, does not exist). ExtraAttributes is not used by Minecraft, but the plugins that the server runs on, which instructs the plugins to display that item in the player's inventory.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Early Days of SkyBlock|Banner was exploited by players.|
Linked from: [[Blacklisted Items]], [[Glitched Items]]

## Obtaining
Barriers can not be obtained by normal means, however, one popular method includes the use of an [[Anvil]].
1.
- Switch to touchscreen controls in your settings.
- Get an item that can be enchanted with an anvil.
- Get an enchanted book that has enchantments that can be applied to your item.
- Drag the anvil inside the UI into your inventory.
- Once the item gets enchanted, drag it into your inventory as well.
This results in barrier named "Anvil" appearing in your inventory. If you hold it in your hand the name switches to "barrier".
2.
- Get a [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] or its refined versions with 2 scrolls applied.
- Apply the third scroll onto it.
- Take the Necron's Blade (or the refined ones) back without closing the anvil menu or shift-clicking.
This results in barrier named "Anvil" appearing in your inventory.
## Description
Barriers are blacklisted. They cannot be stored inside your inventory, backpacks or most containers, and get wiped after about 5–10 seconds. Barriers also can't be stored with [[Item Frame|Item Frames]] or decoration chests.
## Usage
When barriers were introduced, they could be placed on your [[Private Island]]; however, it has since been patched. It is removed from the player inventory after 5-10 seconds.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Barrier was exploited.|
|Unknown Date|Barrier was patched and was no longer obtainable.|
|Jun 9, 2026|Changed rarity from COMMON ([[Rarity]]) to LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]).|
|Jun 9, 2026|Made Barrier Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Jun 9, 2026|The base item of Barrier is now [[Paper]], making it no longer placeable. However, the item model remains [[Barrier]] Barrier.|

# Bat (Private Island)
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
Bat (Private Island) is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Bat (Private Island).
[Bat (Private Island)]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Private Island]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]] [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 100
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Bat Talisman]]
XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: FOREST_ISLAND_BAT_3
Bats are [[Mob|Mobs]] that can spawn on a [[Roofed Forest Island]] on a player's [[Private Island]].
## Spawning
Bats can spawn from a [[Floating Crystals|Bat Crystal]] on [[Roofed Forest Island]] placed on a player's [[Private Island]]. They spawn in a 1 block radius from the crystal. The spawn area must be unblocked.
A Bat spawns every 43 seconds until five are on the island. Spawning continues as long as this cap is not reached.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bat Talisman]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5|5|5|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
During the [[The Great Spook|Great Spook]], they also drop [[Decayed Bat|Decayed Bats]] when killed with a [[Wither Bow]] with [[Arrows|Icy Arrows]]. 5 [[Decayed Bat]] can be brought to the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] to obtain the [[Great Spook Armor|Great Spook Chestplate]].
## Bestiary

## Trivia
- A trapping system can lure Bats to one specific spot for easier killing.
  - A [[Night Saver]] enables constant Bat spawning.
  - Bats can be AFK'd and will still drop [[Bat Talisman]] when drowned (see AFK design).
- The [[Hurricane Bow]] with its ability maxed and the [[Runaan's Bow]] help with hitting Bats as they are very small and fast.

# Beacon Block
Categories: Vanilla objects, Glitches, Common items, Blocks, Craftable items
Beacon Block is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Beacon.
This item or page is a [[Glitched Items|Glitched Item]] or is referencing a [[Glitched Items|Glitched Item]]. More info may be found at the Glitched SkyBlock Items Wiki.
[Beacon Block]
Type: Block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: A cool cosmetic imitation of the real thing!
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BEACON
Tooltip: A cool cosmetic imitation of the real thing!; COMMON
Beacon Block is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that gives players special effects while activated in player's [[Private Island]]. It is the vanilla and [[Glitched Items|unobtainable]] version of the Beacon block in Minecraft, not to be confused with [[Beacon]], which is the official version that unlocks at [[Mithril|Mithril IX (9)]] [[Collections|Collection]].
## Usage
Earlier, Beacon Blocks worked like in vanilla Minecraft. There was a limited supply of Beacon Blocks before the [[Dwarven Mines]] were released.
After several patches, they do not have any usage anymore, other than as decorations.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
Beacon Blocks were previously obtainable by using items with the appearance of a [[Nether Star]] (such as [[Catalyst|Catalysts]] and [[Hyper Catalyst|Hyper Catalysts]]).
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|Nether Star item ([[Catalyst]], [[Hyper Catalyst]], etc.) 3 [[Obsidian]] 5 [[Glass]]|Recipe: [[Glass]] ×5 + [[Catalyst]]/[[Hyper Catalyst]]/[[Eternal Crystal]] + [[Obsidian]] ×3 → Beacon Block|
## Trivia
- To avoid confusion with their tiered counterparts, vanilla Beacons are called "Beacon Blocks" in Hypixel SkyBlock.
- [[Rarity|COMMON]] Beacon Blocks and [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Beacon|Beacons (Numeral)]] consider as two different types of blocks.
- After the Dwarven Mines update Beacon Blocks no longer give any buffs and became a decoration item with the lore of "A cool cosmetic imitation of the real thing!"
- Beacons placed and having an effect selected before the update that disabled such interaction still grants out their effect. This makes it effectively possible to have a few vanilla potion effects, such as Speed, which applies over custom Skyblock effects.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Players started obtaining Beacon Block.|
|Unknown Date|The effect given by Beacon Block was patched.|
Linked from: [[Beacon (disambiguation)]], [[Catalyst]], [[Glitched Items]]

# Blaze
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crimson Isle
Blaze is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Blaze.
[Blaze]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 140
Spawn Location: [[Stronghold]]
Special Behavior: Shoots 5 fireballs at the player.
Mob Type: Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 250,000; [[Damage]]: 2,000
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Blaze Rod]] 0–1x [[Cyclamen Dye]] 0–1x [[Sword of Bad Health]] 0–1x Rampart Armor Piece ([[Rampart Armor]]) 0–1x [[Blaze Ashes]]
XP: +210 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 154
[Metadata]
ID: BLAZE_70
Blazes are [[Mob|Mobs]] found in the [[Crimson Isle]] in the [[Stronghold]] from 5:00 pm to 9:00 am [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]].
## Spawning
Blazes can be found in the [[Stronghold]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Behavior
When idle, Blazes stay on the ground and slowly wander around. When they sense the player, they fly upwards, and try to remain 0.5-3.5 blocks above their target. Blazes attack players by shooting a volley of 6 fireballs, usually when the player is within 16 blocks, but may sometimes start attacking from as far as 24 blocks. They shoot 5 fireballs in a quick succession, 0.2 seconds between each shot. Afterwards, they stop attacking for about 2 seconds.

## Tips
- It is highly recommended to use a [[Moody Grappleshot]] to drag Blazes, preventing them from flying and doubling the [[Damage]] against them.
- Due to Blazes having the ability to fly upwards, it is useful to kill them with a [[Bows|Bow]].
- Because Blazes shoot fireballs that set players on fire, it is recommended to bring a Fire Talisman. It will cause the player to become permanently immune to blazes on their private island, as the fireballs don't deal damage on contact, instead relying on fire damage to kill the player.
- For mid-game players, using at least 2 pieces of [[Terror Armor]], a [[Juju Shortbow]] and preferably a [[Moody Grappleshot]] and/or [[Gauntlet of Contagion]] can be useful for killing a lot of Blazes.
## Trivia
- The description for the blaze in the [[Bestiary]], "They started the fire" is most likely a reference to Billy Joel's 1989 song "We Didn't Start the Fire"

# Block of Coal
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Block of Coal is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Block of Coal.
[Block of Coal]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 9 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: COAL_BLOCK
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; Increases the speed of your minion by 5% for 5; hours!; COMMON
Block of Coal is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Block and [[Minion Fuel]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|9x [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] ×9 → Block of Coal|
|None|9x [[Charcoal]]|Recipe: [[Charcoal]] ×9 → Block of Coal|
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
A Block of Coal can used as [[Minion Fuel]], buffing [[Minions]] by 5% for 5 hours. Block of Coal can be placed in all [[Minions]].
### Private Island
A Block of Coal can be placed as a decorate block on the player's [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]].
### Pure Coal
Breakable Blocks of Coal on [[Mining Islands]] represents [[Ores|Pure Coal]], which has a [[Block Strength]] of 600 and can be broken to obtain [[Coal]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Coal]]|None|[[Block of Coal]] 1x Block of Coal|Recipe: Block of Coal → [[Coal]] x9|
|[[Enchanted Coal]]|Coal IV (4) ([[Coal]])|[[Block of Coal]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Block of Coal|Recipe: Block of Coal x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Coal]] x9|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Block of Coal.|
